الأسطر 115–160

    return f"""## New experiments: design and execution

These are exploratory sensitivity analyses: the original test outcomes were already known. The timestamped protocol was recorded before additional model training; no new test results were used to alter candidates, features, training budgets or reported outcomes. The protocol SHA256 is `{sha256(destination / 'protocol.json')}`. Target definitions, exclusions and budgets are retained in `protocol.json`.

All models retain the exact frozen 30-by-20 feature windows and chronological dates. The primary experiment changes only the label to `delta[t+1] = P[t+1] - P[t]`, then reconstructs `P_hat[t+1] = P[t] + delta_hat[t+1]`. It runs on all five daily assets, retaining the negative oil observation. A separate simple-return experiment uses `r[t+1] = P[t+1]/P[t] - 1` and reconstructs `P_hat[t+1] = P[t] * (1 + r_hat[t+1])` on the four strictly positive daily series. Oil returns are excluded by protocol, with no filtering or clipping of its change experiment. Features still include levels: these experiments do not claim to make the entire input process stationary.

OLS retains training-only scaling/QR rank selection. RF tries 100 trees with depth 5 or 10 and leaf size 3. GBM tries 100 trees with (depth 2, rate .03) or (depth 3, rate .1). Validation RMSE in the **new target units** selects the candidate; return RMSE selection weights errors differently from price RMSE. LSTM uses the original 32 hidden units, dropout .1, Adam .001, gradient clipping 1, at most 60 epochs and patience 8, training-only target/input scalers, no shuffling and identical fixed seed 42. AMP is enabled only if its training-fixture loss check is finite and within 5% of FP32. CUDA/CPU choice, actual precision, batch/VRAM probes, best/last checkpoints, epoch histories, scaler cutoffs, candidates and CPU reload checks are saved for every job. Price predictions are not truncated to historical bounds and returns are not constrained to force plausible results.

Four explicit naive target forecasts are included: zero (exact persistence after reconstruction), training mean (fixed drift), the most recent observed change/return, and the training-majority sign times the training median nonzero magnitude. The latter's magnitude is an explicit forecast convention, not an optimized trading strategy. Last-observation forecasts use the prior label, already known at the current origin; they do not use the unknown current test label. Mean and majority baselines are fitted using training labels only. Zero/drift methods are standard benchmarks; see [Forecasting: Principles and Practice](https://otexts.com/fpp3/simple-methods.html).

### Fixed-split price-change experiment: reconstructed-price RMSE

{rmse_table(delta)}

### Fixed-split simple-return experiment: reconstructed-price RMSE

{rmse_table(returns)}

The OLS change-target forecasts should be almost identical to the original price-target forecasts: with an intercept and the current price in the feature span, subtracting current price from the label and adding it back at prediction is an algebraic reparameterization. A difference in RF/LSTM behavior is therefore informative about representation and model constraints, rather than evidence that changing a target guarantees new information.

The following post-training diagnostic compares LSTM target-prediction dispersion with actual target dispersion. Low reconstructed-price error can arise from forecasting near-zero changes, which is close to persistence, without accurately predicting the day's fluctuation. It must not be presented as evidence that the network recovered a strong return signal. Full amplitudes, means and sample correlations for all models are in `prediction_amplitudes.csv`; these are descriptive, not new selection criteria.

{markdown_table(lstm_amplitudes)}

### Walk-forward validation

Two assets (Apple and S&P 500) receive three expanding-history refits, covering the original 271 test observations in contiguous disjoint blocks of 91, 90 and 90. Before each block, the latest 270 available labels are validation and all earlier labels are training. Each fold refits preprocessing, candidate selection and all four models from scratch. The first fold uses the original training/validation boundaries; later folds can use earlier test labels only once they are available. Forecasts within each block continue to use the actual latest observed feature history; parameters remain frozen until the next block. This is block walk-forward one-step prediction, not a 90-step recursive forecast.

The LSTM walk-forward cap is 30 epochs with patience 8; other settings and tabular candidate budgets are unchanged. The largest actual walk-forward epoch count is {walk_epochs}, with every job's actual device, precision, batch, best epoch and fitted-label cutoff exported in `training_execution_summary.csv`. Validation continues to reserve a 270-observation tail, so these refits reduce staleness incrementally but do not implement daily training or final train-plus-validation refitting. The caps differ from the fixed-split experiment and should not be conflated with a pure refit-frequency causal test. Label availability is explicitly asserted under the daily after-close assumption; general temporal validation motivation is described in [scikit-learn's temporal-split documentation](https://scikit-learn.org/1.5/modules/generated/sklearn.model_selection.TimeSeriesSplit.html).

{rmse_table(walk)}

{markdown_table(walk_intervals)}

### Directional accuracy and uncertainty

{markdown_table(direction)}

Directional accuracy compares three-way signs of predicted and observed price change. Nonzero coverage and accuracy conditional on an active forecast are also saved. Zero-change forecasts have 0% active coverage; their three-way score counts only exactly flat outcomes. They are **not a 0%-skill trading classifier**. Binary up/not-up accuracy is provided separately with zero mapped to not-up. Training-majority direction is the appropriate simple active comparator; 50% is not an assumed empirical baseline. Direction scores do not include trading costs, calibration or profitability.

{markdown_table(intervals)}

Each interval is a paired circular block bootstrap of daily `model squared price error - zero squared price error`, using block length 10, 2,000 resamples and seed 42. Negative differences favor the model. Price RMSE ratios below 1 occur in {len(wins)} of the {len(fixed[fixed.model.isin(LEARNED)])} learned-model/target/asset comparisons; {len(robust)} have an unadjusted interval entirely below zero. Intervals are exploratory, use one prespecified block length, do not include training uncertainty, and have no multiple-comparison correction. Nominal coverage depends on the blocks adequately representing serial dependence; nonstationarity and fold-dependent models can weaken that approximation. Marginal wins or intervals overlapping zero cannot establish reliable superiority. Cross-asset dependence and the shared test period also prevent treating these comparisons as independent replications.

All MAE, RMSE, MAPE, R², direction/coverage, target-unit errors and bootstrap details are in `new_metrics.csv/json`. Price MAPE uses nonzero actual prices with zero counts recorded; target MAPE is also exported for transparency, but near-zero returns/changes make it unstable and it is not a selection criterion. Price R² can remain very high for persistence because the level varies slowly; target R² and error relative to zero change are more informative about incremental forecast skill. No returns, price errors or metrics are pooled across incompatible asset units.
"""

Persistence winning the five daily level-forecast benchmarks is a valid empirical outcome. ## Verification and preservation

Executed {len(verification['jobs'])} additional training jobs, each containing four learned models and four naive baselines. Independently reloaded every learned model on CPU and replayed all {verification['aggregate_rows']:,} saved forecast rows; all {verification['aggregate_metric_rows']} aggregate metric records were recomputed from serialized predictions. Each job has hashes for its models, best/last checkpoints, histories, labels, splits and outputs. One AAPL change-target LSTM was retrained independently: all epoch training/validation losses and all {replication['test_n']} test predictions were bitwise identical on this machine. That is a same-environment reproducibility check, not a multi-seed robustness study.

An initial replay check used a fixed absolute tolerance across target units and failed for two near-zero Nasdaq change predictions. The maximum CPU/CUDA difference was 0.0000076294 index points. Diagnosis then replayed every LSTM on the original CUDA device: all 15 jobs' predictions matched their saved values **bitwise**. CPU differences were at most 0.0000000662 training-target standard deviations, below one FP32 machine epsilon at unit scale. CPU replay now uses an absolute bound of eight FP32 epsilons times the training target standard deviation, with zero relative tolerance; sklearn/baseline replay uses 1e-9 absolute/relative tolerance. This precision-aware verification change does not alter a model or empirical result. The initial failed log and all cross-device measurements remain in `replay_and_preservation.log` and `cross_device_diagnostics.json/log`.

A second verification attempt exposed a distinct, minor arithmetic effect: the LSTM target predictions are native FP32, so NumPy computes `1 + predicted_return` in FP32 before multiplying by the FP64 origin price. Exact replay must preserve that native dtype; casting the saved return to FP64 first produces slightly different implied prices. The failed attempt is retained in `replay_and_preservation_verified.log`; the successful full check is in `replay_and_preservation_final.log`. Both as-executed new result files remain untouched. A separate sensitivity calculation reconstructs **all** saved targets in FP64, without retraining, and saves `float64_reconstructed_predictions.csv`, `float64_reconstructed_metrics.csv` and provenance. Material differences are confined to these four LSTM return jobs:

{markdown_table(precision)}

The largest price-rounding difference is about 0.001394 Nasdaq index points; the largest absolute RMSE difference is about 0.000079 points. No directional predictions change. These are separately labeled numerical sensitivity results, not replacements for the as-executed tables above, and they do not establish learned-model superiority.

The complete automated suite executed {test_suite.attrib['tests']} tests, with {test_suite.attrib['failures']} failures, {test_suite.attrib['errors']} errors and {test_suite.attrib['skipped']} skips. New boundary tests cover return reconstruction/scaling, rejection of negative-price returns, known-label naive forecasts, disjoint walk-forward blocks and directional abstention. Logs and JUnit XML are isolated in this validation directory. See `verification.json`, `tests_final.xml`, `tests_final.log` and `determinism_replication/verification.json`.

All {verification['preservation']['protected_files']:,} files in the pre-review preservation inventory retained their byte counts and SHA256 hashes. This includes the original run and checkpoints, previous result/verification files, input snapshots, thesis, existing source/configuration and verified offline defense package. The original defense ZIP SHA256 remains `{verification['preservation']['offline_zip_sha256']}`. No original result or package was overwritten; this validation is an additive supplement.

New publication exports (PNG and SVG) are in this run's `figures/` directory: `frozen_level_predictions`, `target_representation_rmse`, `paired_uncertainty`, `walk_forward_cumulative_loss`, and `directional_accuracy`. The existing figures and offline package remain untouched. The separate `offline_validation.html` embeds these charts for an offline defense explanation.

يسرد القالب التحقق المسجل وإعادة التحميل والفروق بين الأجهزة والدقة العددية والاختبارات والمحافظة والصور. هذه أوصاف آثار محفوظة من تشغيل سابق وليست دليلاً على تنفيذها في جلسة التوثيق الحالية.

الأسطر 226–238

Suggested oral explanation: “The historical thesis ranks LSTM first but omits the experimental details needed for exact replication. Our transparent next-observation benchmark includes persistence and reveals that level models struggle when test prices rise beyond training support. We verified chronology and scaling, then ran controlled change/return targets and expanding-history validation. These reduce representation-related failures, but the evidence does not justify declaring a universal winner. We report uncertainty and all outcomes rather than tuning to the thesis.”

## Remaining limitations and next research priority

- These analyses reuse an observed test period. A genuinely unseen subsequent period or new prespecified historical holdout is needed for confirmatory comparison; repeatedly choosing models from these test tables would contaminate future claims.
- One seed, one 30-session lookback, two tree candidates, small recurrent networks and fixed epoch budgets do not establish architecture-wide rankings. Stronger regularization, dimensionless relative-price features, train-plus-validation refitting and shorter trailing validation windows are reasonable future experiments, not retrospectively applied fixes.
- Walk-forward covers only two equity series and three refits; validation preserves a 270-session tail. This is a feasibility-bounded sensitivity analysis, not exhaustive daily retraining or cross-market confirmation.
- Yahoo retrospective adjustments, continuous-futures rolls and the negative oil observation limit economic interpretation. Oil change targets remain numerically valid but its extreme training shock affects the loss distribution. Monthly housing lacks vintage/release information and is not a real-time forecasting claim.
- MAPE around zero, level R², directional abstention, unadjusted intervals and cross-asset dependence require the qualifications above. No transaction-cost, trading-profit, probability calibration or causal conclusions are established.
- Existing SHAP/LIME artifacts explain the frozen **level models**. They were preserved and are not relabeled as explanations of newly trained return/change models. Marginal perturbations of correlated financial indicators can be off-manifold; predictive fit and explanation plausibility do not validate human trust or causality. No survey responses were invented.

The next priority is an independently held-out, predeclared comparison of parsimonious relative-price/return features with zero/drift benchmarks and a final-refit policy, followed by multiple seeds and cost-aware trading evaluation if trading claims are intended. The current evidence supports a defensible audit and exploratory comparison, not rewriting the thesis's historical numbers.
"""

يضم نصاً مقترحاً للدفاع وحدوداً: الفترة المعروفة، بذرة وحيدة، نطاق walk-forward، مراجعات البيانات، أخطاء التفسير وغياب دراسة إنسانية وتكاليف التداول. هذه قيود على الاستنتاج ولا تغير النتائج.

import numpy as np

from xai_finance.evaluate.metrics import forecast_metrics

NumPy خارجية وforecast_metrics داخلية لإعادة استعمال مقاييس السعر.

الأسطر 6–10

def directional_statistics(actual_change: np.ndarray, predicted_change: np.ndarray) -> dict:
    actual_sign, predicted_sign = np.sign(actual_change), np.sign(predicted_change)
    active = predicted_sign != 0
    hits = actual_sign == predicted_sign
    return {"direction_accuracy": float(np.mean(hits)), "direction_coverage": float(np.mean(active)), "direction_accuracy_when_active": float(np.mean(hits[active])) if active.any() else None, "binary_up_accuracy_zero_is_not_up": float(np.mean((actual_change > 0) == (predicted_change > 0))), "actual_up_fraction": float(np.mean(actual_change > 0)), "actual_flat_fraction": float(np.mean(actual_change == 0))}

directional_statistics تقارن sign للتغير الفعلي والمتوقع بثلاث حالات، وتحسب نسبة التنبؤ غير الصفري ودقته المشروطة ودقة الصعود الثنائية مع الصفر غير صعود ونسب الصعود والثبات. إذا لم توجد تنبؤات نشطة ترجع None للدقة المشروطة؛ الامتناع لا يساوي بالضرورة قرار هبوط.

الأسطر 13–20

def paired_bootstrap(actual: np.ndarray, predicted: np.ndarray, zero: np.ndarray) -> dict:
    loss_difference = (predicted - actual) ** 2 - (zero - actual) ** 2
    generator = np.random.default_rng(42)
    block_length, draws = 10, 2000
    starts = generator.integers(0, len(actual), size=(draws, int(np.ceil(len(actual) / block_length))))
    indices = ((starts[..., None] + np.arange(block_length)) % len(actual)).reshape(draws, -1)[:, :len(actual)]
    interval = np.quantile(loss_difference[indices].mean(axis=1), [0.025, 0.975])
    return {"mse_difference_vs_zero": float(np.mean(loss_difference)), "mse_difference_ci_low": float(interval[0]), "mse_difference_ci_high": float(interval[1]), "bootstrap_block_length": block_length, "bootstrap_draws": draws, "bootstrap_seed": 42}

paired_bootstrap تحسب dₜ=(ŷₜ−yₜ)²−(zeroₜ−yₜ)² على المشاهدات المزدوجة؛ السالب لصالح النموذج. بمولد seed=42 تختار بدايات 2000 إعادة أخذ كتل دائرية طولها 10 وتلتف modulo n، وتحسب متوسط d لكل عينة وكمّي 2.5% و97.5%. الفرق بوحدة السعر المربعة لا RMSE. الفاصل تقريبي لاستقرار الاعتماد الزمني ولا يشمل عدم يقين إعادة التدريب أو تصحيح تعدد المقارنات؛ الإدخال الفارغ غير صالح.

الأسطر 23–31

def evaluation_metrics(table) -> dict:
    actual, predicted, current = [table[column].to_numpy() for column in ["actual_price", "predicted_price", "current_price"]]
    metrics = forecast_metrics(actual, predicted, current)
    metrics.update(directional_statistics(actual - current, predicted - current))
    metrics.update(paired_bootstrap(actual, predicted, current))
    metrics["rmse_ratio_vs_zero"] = metrics["rmse"] / float(np.sqrt(np.mean((actual - current) ** 2)))
    target_metrics = forecast_metrics(table.actual_target.to_numpy(), table.predicted_target.to_numpy(), np.zeros(len(table)))
    metrics.update({f"target_{name}": target_metrics[name] for name in ["mae", "rmse", "r2", "mape_percent", "mape_excluded_zero_targets"]})
    return metrics

evaluation_metrics تستخرج السعر الحقيقي والمتوقع والحالي، تجمع مقاييس السعر والإشارة وbootstrap مقابل الثبات، ثم نسبة RMSE إلى RMSE الثبات ومقاييس الهدف المحول مقابل صفر. عند مقام ثبات صفري قد تكون النسبة غير معرفة؛ MAPE للأهداف الصغيرة غير مستقر ولا يمثل معيار اختيار هنا.

    dashboard = tmp_path / "dashboard"
    dashboard.mkdir()

import numpy as np
import pytest

from xai_finance.explain.lime_explain import explain_lime

الأسطر 9–19

@pytest.mark.parametrize("model_name", ["LinearRegression", "RandomForest", "GradientBoosting"])
def test_shap_additivity_and_posthoc_explanation_preserve_forecast(window_fixture, model_name):
    model, _ = fit_candidates(candidates({"seed": 42, "trees": 5, "search": False})[model_name], window_fixture)
    observations = window_fixture.flat[window_fixture.partitions["test"][:2]]
    before = model.predict(observations)
    background = window_fixture.flat[window_fixture.partitions["train"][::50]]
    request = {"observations": observations, "names": window_fixture.flat_names, "seed": 42, "cycles": 1}
    explanation = explain_shap(model_name, model, background, request)
    audit = shap_reconstruction(explanation, before)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(before, model.predict(observations))
    assert audit["max_absolute_error"] < 0.01

اختبار معلم لثلاثة نماذج جدولية: يلائم نموذجاً صغيراً على fixture ويحسب تفسير SHAP لعينتين بخلفية تدريبية، ثم يتحقق من الجمع وثبات التوقع وخطأ إعادة البناء أقل من 0.01؛ لا يثبت سببية التفسير.

الأسطر 22–30

def test_lime_accepts_batch_prediction_and_returns_measured_surrogate_error(window_fixture):
    model, _ = fit_candidates(candidates({"seed": 42, "trees": 5, "search": False})["GradientBoosting"], window_fixture)
    training = window_fixture.flat[window_fixture.partitions["train"]]
    observation = window_fixture.flat[window_fixture.partitions["test"][0]]
    before = model.predict(observation[None])
    _, audit = explain_lime(training, model.predict, observation, {"names": window_fixture.flat_names, "seed": 42, "samples": 100})
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(before, model.predict(observation[None]))
    assert audit["absolute_local_error"] == pytest.approx(abs(audit["model_prediction"] - audit["local_surrogate_prediction"]))
    assert np.isfinite(audit["weighted_neighborhood_r2"])

يلائم GradientBoosting صغيراً ويشرح عينة بـ100 اضطراب، ويتحقق من عدم تغيير التنبؤ ومن أن الخطأ هو القيمة المطلقة للفرق بين النموذج والبديل وأن R² الجوار منتهٍ؛ لا يشترط جودة البديل.

@pytest.mark.skipif(not torch.cuda.is_available(), reason="CUDA hardware diagnostic requires an NVIDIA device")
def test_cuda_batch_probe_supports_cudnn_backward():
    """Regression: 2026-10-21 batch probe used eval mode before cuDNN backward."""
    seed_experiment(42)
    network = PriceLSTM({"features": 3, "hidden_size": 4, "dropout": 0.1}).cuda()
    fixture = (torch.randn(8, 5, 3, device="cuda"), torch.randn(8, device="cuda"))
    measurement = measure_batch(network, fixture, torch.cuda.mem_get_info()[0])
    assert measurement["samples_per_second"] > 0
    assert all(torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all() for parameter in network.parameters())

الأسطر 20–28

def test_cpu_lstm_training_checkpoint_and_inverse_scale_roundtrip(window_fixture, tmp_path):
    seed_experiment(42)
    configuration = {"device": "cpu", "hidden_size": 4, "dropout": 0.1, "batch_candidates": [16, 32], "amp": False, "learning_rate": 0.001, "epochs": 2, "patience": 2, "effective_lookback": 30}
    model, audit = fit_lstm(window_fixture, configuration, tmp_path)
    reloaded = load_lstm(tmp_path, model.scalers, "cpu")
    heldout = window_fixture.flat[window_fixture.partitions["test"][:3]]
    np.testing.assert_allclose(model.predict(heldout), reloaded.predict(heldout), atol=1e-6)
    assert np.isfinite(model.predict(heldout)).all()
    assert audit["device"] == "cpu"

يدرب نموذج CPU صغيراً لحد epochين في مجلد مؤقت، ويحمل checkpoint ويقارن تنبؤ ثلاث نوافذ ويتحقق من النتائج المنتهية والجهاز. هذا اختبار اصطناعي لا تدريب البحث المحفوظ.

def test_repeated_forest_inference_is_bitwise_stable(window_fixture):
    """Regression: parallel tree accumulation changed last bits during XAI checks."""
    model, _ = fit_candidates(candidates({"seed": 42, "trees": 40, "search": False})["RandomForest"], window_fixture)
    observations = window_fixture.flat[window_fixture.partitions["test"]]
    expected = model.predict(observations)

@pytest.mark.parametrize("model_name", ["LinearRegression", "RandomForest", "GradientBoosting"])
def test_saved_tabular_model_preserves_predictions_without_refitting(window_fixture, tmp_path, model_name):
    estimators = candidates({"seed": 42, "trees": 5, "search": False})[model_name]
    model, audit = fit_candidates(estimators, window_fixture)
    test = window_fixture.partitions["test"]
    before = model.predict(window_fixture.flat[test])
    joblib.dump(model, tmp_path / "model.joblib")
    reloaded = joblib.load(tmp_path / "model.joblib")
    np.testing.assert_allclose(before, reloaded.predict(window_fixture.flat[test]), atol=1e-8)
    assert audit["trials"][0]["validation_rmse"] >= 0

اختبار معلم لثلاثة نماذج يلائم ويحفظ مؤقتاً ويعيد تحميل joblib ويقارن التنبؤ ويتحقق من RMSE تحقق غير سالب.

import numpy as np

from xai_finance.data.windows import build_windows

الأسطر 11–22

@pytest.mark.integration
def test_real_provider_ols_explanation_reconstructs_stably():
    """Regression: provider-rounded dependent features caused catastrophic OLS cancellation."""
    prices, _ = load_snapshot("AAPL")
    windows = build_windows(features(prices, "daily"), 30)
    model, _ = fit_candidates(candidates({"seed": 42, "trees": 5, "search": False})["LinearRegression"], windows)
    indices = windows.partitions["test"][np.linspace(0, len(windows.partitions["test"]) - 1, 10, dtype=int)]
    background = windows.flat[windows.partitions["train"][np.linspace(0, len(windows.partitions["train"]) - 1, 8, dtype=int)]]
    request = {"observations": windows.flat[indices], "names": windows.flat_names, "seed": 42, "cycles": 1}
    explanation = explain_shap("LinearRegression", model, background, request)
    audit = shap_reconstruction(explanation, model.predict(windows.flat[indices]))
    assert audit["max_absolute_error"] < 1e-3

اختبار integration يحمل لقطة AAPL المحلية، ويبني النوافذ ويلائم OLS على التدريب، ويختار 10 اختبارات و8 خلفيات ويفحص إعادة بناء SHAP بأقل من 1e-3. يتطلب لقطة محفوظة ويدرّب نموذج اختبار؛ لا يثبت إعادة إنتاج الرسالة.

@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", ["price_change", "simple_return"])
def test_target_reconstruction_and_scaling_restore_next_price(window_fixture, target):
    transformed = transformed_windows(window_fixture, target)
    scalers = fit_training_scalers(transformed)
    standardized = scalers.target.transform(transformed.y[:, None])
    restored = implied_prices(scalers.inverse_target(standardized), window_fixture.current_close, target)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(restored, window_fixture.y, atol=1e-12)
    assert transformed.x is window_fixture.x
    assert not np.shares_memory(transformed.y, window_fixture.y)

لكل هدف تغير أو عائد يختبر التحويل والتقييس والعكس إلى السعر التالي، وأن x مشترك وy الجديد لا يشارك الذاكرة.

الأسطر 22–28

def test_return_protocol_rejects_negative_price_but_changes_preserve_it(window_fixture):
    prices = window_fixture.current_close.copy()
    prices[0] = -1
    altered = replace(window_fixture, current_close=prices)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="strictly positive"):
        target_values(altered, "simple_return")
    np.testing.assert_allclose(implied_prices(target_values(altered, "price_change"), prices, "price_change"), altered.y)

يضع سعراً سالباً صناعياً ويتوقع رفض العائد، مع إمكان إعادة تركيب السعر من التغير.

الأسطر 31–38

def test_walk_forward_blocks_are_disjoint_and_fit_only_available_labels(window_fixture):
    partitions = walk_forward_partitions(window_fixture, 3)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(np.concatenate([partition["test"] for partition in partitions]), window_fixture.partitions["test"])
    for partition in partitions:
        assert np.intersect1d(partition["train"], partition["validation"]).size == 0
        assert window_fixture.targets[partition["train"][-1]] <= window_fixture.origins[partition["validation"][0]]
        assert window_fixture.targets[partition["validation"][-1]] <= window_fixture.origins[partition["test"][0]]
    assert len(partitions[-1]["train"]) > len(partitions[0]["train"])

يتحقق من تغطية طيات الاختبار المتصلة دون تداخل وتوافر تسميات التدريب والتحقق وزيادة طول التدريب مع الطيات؛ لا يختبر أزمنة نشر البيانات.

الأسطر 41–52

@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", ["price_change", "simple_return"])
def test_naive_predictions_do_not_use_unknown_test_labels(window_fixture, target):
    before = naive_predictions(window_fixture, target)
    test = window_fixture.partitions["test"]
    changed_y = window_fixture.y.copy()
    changed_y[test] *= 2
    altered = replace(window_fixture, y=changed_y)
    after = naive_predictions(altered, target)
    for name in ["Zero", "TrainMean", "TrainMajorityDirectionMedianMagnitude"]:
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(before[name], after[name])
    assert before["LastObservedChangeOrReturn"][0] == after["LastObservedChangeOrReturn"][0]
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(before["LastObservedChangeOrReturn"], target_values(window_fixture, target)[test - 1])

يغير أهداف الاختبار ويقارن المراجع الثلاثة الثابتة والهدف السابق لأول عينة؛ يثبت أن LastObserved لكل أصل يستخدم الهدف السابق، الذي قد يكون هدف اختبار سابقاً أصبح معروفاً.

def test_zero_prediction_is_directional_abstention():
    metrics = directional_statistics(np.array([1.0, -1.0, 0.0]), np.zeros(3))
    assert metrics["direction_accuracy"] == pytest.approx(1 / 3)
    assert metrics["direction_coverage"] == 0
    assert metrics["direction_accuracy_when_active"] is None
    assert metrics["binary_up_accuracy_zero_is_not_up"] == pytest.approx(2 / 3)

@pytest.mark.parametrize("frequency", ["daily", "monthly"])
def test_future_price_mutation_preserves_past_features(analytic_prices, frequency):
    prices = analytic_prices if frequency == "daily" else analytic_prices[["close"]]
    cutoff = prices.index[190]
    changed = prices.copy()
    changed.loc[changed.index > cutoff] *= 20
    original = features(prices, frequency)
    mutated = features(changed, frequency)
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(original.loc[:cutoff], mutated.loc[:cutoff])

يغير الأسعار بعد حد زمني لعينة يومية وشهرية ويتحقق من ثبات السمات السابقة؛ لا يثبت سلامة الإصدارات التاريخية للمزود.

الأسطر 23–31

def test_targets_are_exactly_next_observed_close_and_windows_end_at_origin(analytic_prices):
    engineered = features(analytic_prices, "daily")
    windows = build_windows(engineered, 30)
    next_dates = analytic_prices.index[analytic_prices.index.get_indexer(windows.origins) + 1]
    assert windows.targets.equals(next_dates)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(windows.y, analytic_prices.loc[next_dates, "adj_close"])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(windows.x[:, -1, 0], analytic_prices.loc[windows.origins, "adj_close"])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(windows.x[0], engineered.iloc[:30])
    assert (windows.origins < windows.targets).all()

يتحقق أن الهدف هو الإغلاق المعدل للرصد الخام التالي وأن نهاية كل نافذة عند الأصل، وأن أول نافذة تتطابق مع أول ثلاثين صف سمة.

الأسطر 34–42

def test_split_targets_never_cross_partition_boundary(window_fixture):
    partitions = window_fixture.partitions
    count = len(window_fixture.y)
    assert len(partitions["train"]) == int(0.8 * count)
    assert len(partitions["validation"]) == int(0.1 * count)
    assert sum(len(indices) for indices in partitions.values()) == count
    for earlier, later in [("train", "validation"), ("validation", "test")]:
        assert window_fixture.targets[partitions[earlier][-1]] < window_fixture.targets[partitions[later][0]]
        assert window_fixture.targets[partitions[earlier][-1]] <= window_fixture.origins[partitions[later][0]]

يتحقق من حجم التدريب والتحقق ومجموع الأقسام وترتيب تواريخ الأهداف وإتاحة التسمية السابقة عند أصل القسم التالي.

الأسطر 45–53

def test_heldout_mutation_cannot_change_fitted_scalers(window_fixture):
    before = fit_training_scalers(window_fixture)
    validation_and_test = np.r_[window_fixture.partitions["validation"], window_fixture.partitions["test"]]
    window_fixture.x[validation_and_test] *= 1000
    window_fixture.y[validation_and_test] *= 1000
    after = fit_training_scalers(window_fixture)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(before.inputs.mean_, after.inputs.mean_)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(before.target.mean_, after.target.mean_)
    assert before.fitted_target_cutoff < str(window_fixture.targets[validation_and_test[0]].date())

يضرب نوافذ وأهداف التحقق والاختبار في 1000 ثم يعيد ملاءمة المعايير، ويتأكد من ثبات متوسطَي التدريب وسبق آخر هدف تدريب؛ يعدل fixture مؤقتاً.

الأسطر 56–59

def test_target_inverse_transform_restores_original_units(window_fixture):
    scalers = fit_training_scalers(window_fixture)
    normalized = scalers.target.transform(window_fixture.y[:, None])
    np.testing.assert_allclose(scalers.inverse_target(normalized), window_fixture.y, rtol=1e-12)

يتحقق من أن معيار الهدف وعكسه يسترجعان الوحدات الأصلية ضمن rtol محدد.

الأسطر 62–73

@pytest.mark.parametrize("fault", ["duplicate", "reversed", "bad_high", "nan"])
def test_invalid_provider_snapshot_is_rejected(analytic_prices, fault):
    if fault == "duplicate":
        analytic_prices = pd.concat([analytic_prices, analytic_prices.iloc[[-1]]])
    elif fault == "reversed":
        analytic_prices = analytic_prices.iloc[::-1]
    elif fault == "bad_high":
        analytic_prices.iloc[0, analytic_prices.columns.get_loc("high")] = 0
    else:
        analytic_prices.iloc[0, 0] = np.nan
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_prices(analytic_prices, "daily")

يحقن تكرار تاريخ أو ترتيباً معكوساً أو high خاطئاً أو NaN ويتوقع ValueError من تحقق الأسعار.

def test_insufficient_history_fails_instead_of_empty_test_set():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Too few"):
        chronological_indices(5)
